{"parentUuid":"9d48b6af-2084-4427-82a4-cf61542eb325","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01MLtzw5ey6Jnns4LefQewaK","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01PfT9ZiirPWSnxR4cB6ZzT2","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\bash-hook-lint-fire-test.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for bash-hook-lint.sh patterns A/B/C/D extension (Phase 3.5 T3.4 + S54 refinement per L-S51-1).\n#\n# Stages synthetic hook scripts (dirty + clean) into a tempdir scripts/hooks/ layout,\n# runs bash-hook-lint pointed at the tempdir, and asserts:\n#   Check 5 — Pattern A (M-S51-1) emits on imagined-format grep\n#   Check 6 — Pattern B (L-S48m-1) emits on $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID marker\n#   Check 7 — Pattern C (L-S48d-1) emits on pipefail+ERR-trap+bare-grep:\n#     TC-C  : bare-line grep (orig fixture)\n#     TC-C2 : command-substitution `VAR=\"$(grep ...)\"` form (S54 KI-S53-1; was missed pre-S54)\n#     TC-C3 : pipeline `grep ... | other ...` form (S54 refinement)\n#   Check 7 negative — `if grep`, already-guarded `|| true`:\n#     TC-C-cond : `if grep ...; then` form NOT flagged\n#     TC-C-guard: `$(grep ... || true)` NOT flagged\n#   Check 8 — Pattern D (L-S53-2 unanchored positional-marker grep, NEW S54):\n#     TC-D  : unanchored `grep -E 'S[0-9]+ NEXT'` flagged\n#     TC-D-anchored : anchored `grep -E '^S[0-9]+ NEXT'` NOT flagged\n#     TC-D-content : arbitrary content `grep \"foo bar\"` NOT flagged\n#   TC-clean — pure clean hook NOT flagged for any check.\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -uo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../bash-hook-lint.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap 'rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR\"' EXIT\n\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks\"\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n\n# === Pattern A (Check 5): M-S51-1 imagined-format grep (literal **Session N**) ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/imagined-format-grep.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -u\nEXEC_FILE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\nLATEST_SESSION=$(grep -E '^\\*\\*Session N\\*\\*:' \"$EXEC_FILE\" | head -1 | sed 's/.*: //' || true)\necho \"$LATEST_SESSION\"\nEOF\n\n# === Pattern B (Check 6): L-S48m-1 CLAUDE_SESSION_ID marker without fallback ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/claude-session-id-marker.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -u\nMARKER_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nMARKER=\"${MARKER_DIR}/.foo-fired-${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID}\"\n[ -f \"$MARKER\" ] && exit 0\ntouch \"$MARKER\"\nEOF\n\n# === Pattern C (Check 7) — TC-C: bare-line grep (orig fixture) ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/pipefail-err-trap-bare-grep.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/log.txt\"\n    grep -m1 -E '^session=' \"$LOG\"\necho \"after grep\"\nEOF\n\n# === Pattern C (Check 7) — TC-C2: command-substitution form (S54 KI-S53-1 regression) ===\n# This is the EXACT shape of session-export-raw.sh:42 pre-S53. Pre-S54 Check 7 missed it.\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/pipefail-cmd-sub-grep.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\nEXEC_FILE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/exec.md\"\nSESSION_N=\"$(grep -E '^foo' \"$EXEC_FILE\" | grep -oE '[0-9]+' | sort -n | tail -1)\"\necho \"$SESSION_N\"\nEOF\n\n# === Pattern C (Check 7) — TC-C3: pipeline grep at start, no || true ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/pipefail-pipeline-grep.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/log.txt\"\ngrep -E 'pat' \"$LOG\" | head -1 | sed 's/foo/bar/'\necho \"done\"\nEOF\n\n# === Pattern C negative — TC-C-cond: `if grep` form NOT flagged ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/pipefail-if-grep.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/log.txt\"\nif grep -qE 'foo' \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"found\"\nfi\nEOF\n\n# === Pattern C negative — TC-C-guard: $(grep ... || true) form NOT flagged ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/pipefail-grep-guarded.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/log.txt\"\nRESULT=\"$(grep -E 'foo' \"$LOG\" | head -1 || true)\"\necho \"$RESULT\"\nEOF\n\n# === Pattern D (Check 8) — TC-D: unanchored positional-marker grep ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/unanchored-positional-grep.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -u\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/log.txt\"\nRESULT=$(grep -E 'S[0-9]+[[:space:]]+NEXT' \"$LOG\" | head -1 || true)\necho \"$RESULT\"\nEOF\n\n# === Pattern D negative — TC-D-anchored: ^-anchored positional-marker grep NOT flagged ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/anchored-positional-grep.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -u\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/log.txt\"\nRESULT=$(grep -E '^S[0-9]+[[:space:]]+NEXT' \"$LOG\" | head -1 || true)\nHEADER=$(grep -oE '^## S[0-9]+' \"$LOG\" | sort | tail -1 || true)\necho \"$RESULT $HEADER\"\nEOF\n\n# === Pattern D negative — TC-D-content: arbitrary content grep NOT flagged ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/content-grep.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -u\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/log.txt\"\nRESULT=$(grep \"foo bar\" \"$LOG\" | head -1 || true)\nCOUNT=$(grep -c \"warning\" \"$LOG\" || true)\necho \"$RESULT $COUNT\"\nEOF\n\n# === TC-clean: clean hook NOT flagged for any check ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/clean-hook.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -u\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/log.txt\"\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\"\nprintf '[%s] hello\\n' \"$(date)\" >> \"$LOG\"\nexit 0\nEOF\n\nchmod +x \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/\"*.sh\n\n# Run bash-hook-lint pointed at tempdir\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n[ ! -f \"$LOG\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: session-hooks.log not created\"; exit 1; }\n\nPASS=0; FAIL=0\n\nassert_flagged() {\n  # $1=label, $2=violation_code_regex, $3=hook_basename\n  if grep -qE \"${2}.*${3}\" \"$LOG\"; then\n    echo \"PASS [${1}] ${2} detected on ${3}\"\n    PASS=$((PASS+1))\n  else\n    echo \"FAIL [${1}] ${2} NOT detected on ${3}\"\n    echo \"--- relevant log lines ---\"\n    grep -E \"${3}|${2}\" \"$LOG\" || echo \"(no matches)\"\n    echo \"--------------------------\"\n    FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\n  fi\n}\n\nassert_NOT_flagged() {\n  # $1=label, $2=violation_code_regex, $3=hook_basename\n  if grep -qE \"${2}.*${3}\" \"$LOG\"; then\n    echo \"FAIL [${1}] false-positive: ${2} flagged on ${3}\"\n    grep -E \"${3}\" \"$LOG\" || true\n    FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\n  else\n    echo \"PASS [${1}] no false-positive: ${2} NOT on ${3}\"\n    PASS=$((PASS+1))\n  fi\n}\n\n# Check 5 — Pattern A\nassert_flagged \"TC-A\" \"M-S51-1-IMAGINED-FORMAT\" \"imagined-format-grep\"\n\n# Check 6 — Pattern B\nassert_flagged \"TC-B\" \"L-S48m-1-CLAUDE-SESSION-ID-MARKER\" \"claude-session-id-marker\"\n\n# Check 7 — Pattern C (refined S54)\nassert_flagged \"TC-C\"  \"L-S48d-1-PIPEFAIL-BARE-GREP\" \"pipefail-err-trap-bare-grep\"\nassert_flagged \"TC-C2\" \"L-S48d-1-PIPEFAIL-BARE-GREP\" \"pipefail-cmd-sub-grep\"\nassert_flagged \"TC-C3\" \"L-S48d-1-PIPEFAIL-BARE-GREP\" \"pipefail-pipeline-grep\"\n\n# Check 7 negative\nassert_NOT_flagged \"TC-C-cond\"  \"L-S48d-1-PIPEFAIL-BARE-GREP\" \"pipefail-if-grep\"\nassert_NOT_flagged \"TC-C-guard\" \"L-S48d-1-PIPEFAIL-BARE-GREP\" \"pipefail-grep-guarded\"\n\n# Check 8 — Pattern D (NEW S54)\nassert_flagged \"TC-D\" \"L-S53-2-UNANCHORED-POSITIONAL-GREP\" \"unanchored-positional-grep\"\n\n# Check 8 negative\nassert_NOT_flagged \"TC-D-anchored\" \"L-S53-2-UNANCHORED-POSITIONAL-GREP\" \"anchored-positional-grep\"\nassert_NOT_flagged \"TC-D-content\"  \"L-S53-2-UNANCHORED-POSITIONAL-GREP\" \"content-grep\"\n\n# TC-clean — no violations on clean-hook.sh for ANY check\nif grep -qE \"(M-S51-1|L-S48m-1|L-S48d-1|L-S53-2).*clean-hook\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL [TC-clean] false-positive on clean-hook.sh\"\n  grep \"clean-hook\" \"$LOG\" || true\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nelse\n  echo \"PASS [TC-clean] no false-positive on clean-hook.sh\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nfi\n\necho \"\"\nprintf '=== TOTAL: PASS=%d FAIL=%d ===\\n' \"$PASS\" \"$FAIL\"\n[ \"$FAIL\" -eq 0 ] && exit 0 || exit 1\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1430,"cache_read_input_tokens":134426,"output_tokens":5284,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1430,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":5284,"cache_read_input_tokens":134426,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1430,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1430},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CajWhTvutWQte2F7ZtQqP","type":"assistant","uuid":"955abe50-d68a-42d0-b2c6-a77368a476a5","timestamp":"2026-05-05T13:37:36.020Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"6fa1c8c7-7014-4741-9fb0-2edf9459f4f1","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

then\n  echo \"found\"\nfi\nEOF\n\n# === Pattern C negative — TC-C-guard: $(grep ... || true) form NOT flagged ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/pipefail-grep-guarded.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/log.txt\"\nRESULT=\"$(grep -E 'foo' \"$LOG\" | head -1 || true)\"\necho \"$RESULT\"\nEOF\n\n# === Pattern D (Check 8) — TC-D: unanchored positional-marker grep ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/unanchored-positional-grep.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -u\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/log.txt\"\nRESULT=$(grep -E 'S[0-9]+[[:space:]]+NEXT' \"$LOG\" | head -1 || true)\necho \"$RESULT\"\nEOF\n\n# === Pattern D negative — TC-D-anchored: ^-anchored positional-marker grep NOT flagged ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/anchored-positional-grep.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -u\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/log.txt\"\nRESULT=$(grep -E '^S[0-9]+[[:space:]]+NEXT' \"$LOG\" | head -1 || true)\nHEADER=$(grep -oE '^## S[0-9]+' \"$LOG\" | sort | tail -1 || true)\necho \"$RESULT $HEADER\"\nEOF\n\n# === Pattern D negative — TC-D-content: arbitrary content grep NOT flagged ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/content-grep.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -u\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/log.txt\"\nRESULT=$(grep \"foo bar\" \"$LOG\" | head -1 || true)\nCOUNT=$(grep -c \"warning\" \"$LOG\" || true)\necho \"$RESULT $COUNT\"\nEOF\n\n# === TC-clean: clean hook NOT flagged for any check ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/clean-hook.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -u\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/log.txt\"\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\"\nprintf '[%s] hello\\n' \"$(date)\" >> \"$LOG\"\nexit 0\nEOF\n\nchmod +x \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/\"*.sh\n\n# Run bash-hook-lint pointed at tempdir\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n[ ! -f \"$LOG\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: session-hooks.log not created\"; exit 1; }\n\nPASS=0; FAIL=0\n\nassert_flagged() {\n  # $1=label, $2=violation_code_regex, $3=hook_basename\n  if grep -qE \"${2}.*${3}\" \"$LOG\"; then\n    echo \"PASS [${1}] ${2} detected on ${3}\"\n    PASS=$((PASS+1))\n  else\n    echo \"FAIL [${1}] ${2} NOT detected on ${3}\"\n    echo \"--- relevant log lines ---\"\n    grep -E \"${3}|${2}\" \"$LOG\" || echo \"(no matches)\"\n    echo \"--------------------------\"\n    FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\n  fi\n}\n\nassert_NOT_flagged() {\n  # $1=label, $2=violation_code_regex, $3=hook_basename\n  if grep -qE \"${2}.*${3}\" \"$LOG\"; then\n    echo \"FAIL [${1}] false-positive: ${2} flagged on ${3}\"\n    grep -E \"${3}\" \"$LOG\" || true\n    FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\n  else\n    echo \"PASS [${1}] no false-positive: ${2} NOT on ${3}\"\n    PASS=$((PASS+1))\n  fi\n}\n\n# Check 5 — Pattern A\nassert_flagged \"TC-A\" \"M-S51-1-IMAGINED-FORMAT\" \"imagined-format-grep\"\n\n# Check 6 — Pattern B\nassert_flagged \"TC-B\" \"L-S48m-1-CLAUDE-SESSION-ID-MARKER\" \"claude-session-id-marker\"\n\n# Check 7 — Pattern C (refined S54)\nassert_flagged \"TC-C\"  \"L-S48d-1-PIPEFAIL-BARE-GREP\" \"pipefail-err-trap-bare-grep\"\nassert_flagged \"TC-C2\" \"L-S48d-1-PIPEFAIL-BARE-GREP\" \"pipefail-cmd-sub-grep\"\nassert_flagged \"TC-C3\" \"L-S48d-1-PIPEFAIL-BARE-GREP\" \"pipefail-pipeline-grep\"\n\n# Check 7 negative\nassert_NOT_flagged \"TC-C-cond\"  \"L-S48d-1-PIPEFAIL-BARE-GREP\" \"pipefail-if-grep\"\nassert_NOT_flagged \"TC-C-guard\" \"L-S48d-1-PIPEFAIL-BARE-GREP\" \"pipefail-grep-guarded\"\n\n# Check 8 — Pattern D (NEW S54)\nassert_flagged \"TC-D\" \"L-S53-2-UNANCHORED-POSITIONAL-GREP\" \"unanchored-positional-grep\"\n\n# Check 8 negative\nassert_NOT_flagged \"TC-D-anchored\" \"L-S53-2-UNANCHORED-POSITIONAL-GREP\" \"anchored-positional-grep\"\nassert_NOT_flagged \"TC-D-content\"  \"L-S53-2-UNANCHORED-POSITIONAL-GREP\" \"content-grep\"\n\n# TC-clean — no violations on clean-hook.sh for ANY check\nif grep -qE \"(M-S51-1|L-S48m-1|L-S48d-1|L-S53-2).*clean-hook\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL [TC-clean] false-positive on clean-hook.sh\"\n  grep \"clean-hook\" \"$LOG\" || true\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nelse\n  echo \"PASS [TC-clean] no false-positive on clean-hook.sh\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nfi\n\necho \"\"\nprintf '=== TOTAL: PASS=%d FAIL=%d ===\\n' \"$PASS\" \"$FAIL\"\n[ \"$FAIL\" -eq 0 ] && exit 0 || exit 1\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":12,"newStart":1,"newLines":22,"lines":[" #!/usr/bin/env bash","-# Firing-test for bash-hook-lint.sh patterns A/B/C extension (Phase 3.5 T3.4 Cluster 6 per L-S51-1).","+# Firing-test for bash-hook-lint.sh patterns A/B/C/D extension (Phase 3.5 T3.4 + S54 refinement per L-S51-1)."," #","-# Stages 4 synthetic hook scripts (3 dirty + 1 clean) into a tempdir scripts/hooks/ layout,","+# Stages synthetic hook scripts (dirty + clean) into a tempdir scripts/hooks/ layout,"," # runs bash-hook-lint pointed at the tempdir, and asserts:","-#   - Pattern A (M-S51-1) emits on imagined-format grep","-#   - Pattern B (L-S48m-1) emits on $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID marker","-#   - Pattern C (L-S48d-1) emits on pipefail+ERR-trap+bare-grep","-#   - Clean hook is NOT flagged (no false-positive)","+#   Check 5 — Pattern A (M-S51-1) emits on imagined-format grep","+#   Check 6 — Pattern B (L-S48m-1) emits on $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID marker","+#   Check 7 — Pattern C (L-S48d-1) emits on pipefail+ERR-trap+bare-grep:","+#     TC-C  : bare-line grep (orig fixture)","+#     TC-C2 : command-substitution `VAR=\"$(grep ...)\"` form (S54 KI-S53-1; then","   echo \"FAIL [TC-clean] false-positive on clean-hook.sh\"","   grep \"clean-hook\" \"$LOG\" || true","   FAIL=$((FAIL+1))"]}],"originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for bash-hook-lint.sh patterns A/B/C extension (Phase 3.5 T3.4 Cluster 6 per L-S51-1).\n#\n# Stages 4 synthetic hook scripts (3 dirty + 1 clean) into a tempdir scripts/hooks/ layout,\n# runs bash-hook-lint pointed at the tempdir, and asserts:\n#   - Pattern A (M-S51-1) emits on imagined-format grep\n#   - Pattern B (L-S48m-1) emits on $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID marker\n#   - Pattern C (L-S48d-1) emits on pipefail+ERR-trap+bare-grep\n#   - Clean hook is NOT flagged (no false-positive)\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -uo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../bash-hook-lint.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap 'rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR\"' EXIT\n\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks\"\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n\n# Pattern A: M-S51-1 imagined-format grep (literal **Session N**)\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/imagined-format-grep.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -u\nEXEC_FILE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\nLATEST_SESSION=$(grep -E '^\\*\\*Session N\\*\\*:' \"$EXEC_FILE\" | head -1 | sed 's/.*: //' || true)\necho \"$LATEST_SESSION\"\nEOF\n\n# Pattern B: L-S48m-1 CLAUDE_SESSION_ID marker without fallback\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/claude-session-id-marker.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -u\nMARKER_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nMARKER=\"${MARKER_DIR}/.foo-fired-${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID}\"\n[ -f \"$MARKER\" ] && exit 0\ntouch \"$MARKER\"\nEOF\n\n# Pattern C: L-S48d-1 pipefail + ERR-trap + bare grep (no || true)\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/pipefail-err-trap-bare-grep.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/log.txt\"\n    grep -m1 -E '^session=' \"$LOG\"\necho \"after grep\"\nEOF\n\n# Clean hook (negative case)\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/clean-hook.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -u\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/log.txt\"\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\"\nprintf '[%s] hello\\n' \"$(date)\" >> \"$LOG\"\nexit 0\nEOF\n\nchmod +x \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/\"*.sh\n\n# Run bash-hook-lint pointed at tempdir\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n[ ! -f \"$LOG\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: session-hooks.log not created\"; exit 1; }\n\nPASS=0; FAIL=0\n\n# TC-A: Pattern A (M-S51-1) flagged on imagined-format-grep.sh\nif grep -q \"M-S51-1-IMAGINED-FORMAT.*imagined-format-grep\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"PASS [TC-A] M-S51-1 detected on imagined-format-grep.sh\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL [TC-A] M-S51-1 NOT detected\"\n  echo \"--- log content ---\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  echo \"-------------------\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# TC-B: Pattern B (L-S48m-1) flagged on claude-session-id-marker.sh\nif grep -q \"L-S48m-1-CLAUDE-SESSION-ID-MARKER.*claude-session-id-marker\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"PASS [TC-B] L-S48m-1 detected on claude-session-id-marker.sh\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL [TC-B] L-S48m-1 NOT detected\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# TC-C: Pattern C (L-S48d-1) flagged on pipefail-err-trap-bare-grep.sh\nif grep -q \"L-S48d-1-PIPEFAIL-BARE-GREP.*pipefail-err-trap-bare-grep\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"PASS [TC-C] L-S48d-1 detected on pipefail-err-trap-bare-grep.sh\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nelse\n  echo \"FAIL [TC-C] L-S48d-1 NOT detected\"\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nfi\n\n# TC-clean: no violations on clean-hook.sh\nif grep -qE \"(M-S51-1|L-S48m-1|L-S48d-1).*clean-hook\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL [TC-clean] false-positive on clean-hook.sh\"\n  grep \"clean-hook\" \"$LOG\" || true\n  FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\nelse\n  echo \"PASS [TC-clean] no false-positive on clean-hook.sh\"\n  PASS=$((PASS+1))\nfi\n\necho \"\"\nprintf '=== TOTAL: PASS=%d FAIL=%d ===\\n' \"$PASS\" \"$FAIL\"\n[ \"$FAIL\" -eq 0 ] && exit 0 || exit 1\n","userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"955abe50-d68a-42d0-b2c6-a77368a476a5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"6fa1c8c7-7014-4741-9fb0-2edf9459f4f1","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"d738e6c6-7dc2-4091-9abb-3a75fd090a98","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01Kn32mNaUiQkkdek1nj7Cpc","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01RvysAvdZVSkR1iBxLDuDY6","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\bash-hook-lint.sh","old_string":"# === Check 8: L-S53-2 unanchored positional-marker grep (NEW Pattern D) — S54 ===\n# Origin: S53 M-S53-2 — `session-export-raw.sh` Method 1 grep `'S[0-9]+[[:space:]]+NEXT'`\n# without ^ anchor matched archive prose mid-line \"S38/S42 NEXT branching gate\" →\n# returned false-positive 42 every export, silently corrupted ALL raw-session filenames.\n# Detection: grep patterns containing routing-marker regex tokens (S[0-9]+, Track [0-9]+,\n# Session N, Session [0-9]+, ## S[0-9]+) where the pattern body does NOT begin with `^`.\n# Whitelist via `[^^'\\\"]` (first body char must NOT be caret).\nif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n    [ ! then\n    echo \"FAIL [${1}] false-positive: ${2} flagged on ${3}\"\n    grep -E \": ${3}\\.sh\" \"$LOG\" || true\n    FAIL=$((FAIL+1))\n  else\n    echo \"PASS [${1}] no false-positive: ${2} NOT on ${3}\"\n    PASS=$((PASS+1))\n  fi\n}","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for bash-hook-lint.sh patterns A/B/C/D extension (Phase 3.5 T3.4 + S54 refinement per L-S51-1).\n#\n# Stages synthetic hook scripts (dirty + clean) into a tempdir scripts/hooks/ layout,\n# runs bash-hook-lint pointed at the tempdir, and asserts:\n#   Check 5 — Pattern A (M-S51-1) emits on imagined-format grep\n#   Check 6 — Pattern B (L-S48m-1) emits on $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID marker\n#   Check 7 — Pattern C (L-S48d-1) emits on pipefail+ERR-trap+bare-grep:\n#     TC-C  : bare-line grep (orig fixture)\n#     TC-C2 : command-substitution `VAR=\"$(grep ...)\"` form (S54 KI-S53-1; was missed pre-S54)\n#     TC-C3 : pipeline `grep ... | other ...` form (S54 refinement)\n#   Check 7 negative — `if grep`, already-guarded `|| true`:\n#     TC-C-cond : `if grep ...; then` form NOT flagged\n#     TC-C-guard: `$(grep ... || true)` NOT flagged\n#   Check 8 — Pattern D (L-S53-2 unanchored positional-marker grep, NEW S54):\n#     TC-D  : unanchored `grep -E 'S[0-9]+ NEXT'` flagged\n#     TC-D-anchored : anchored `grep -E '^S[0-9]+ NEXT'` NOT flagged\n#     TC-D-content : arbitrary content `grep \"foo bar\"` NOT flagged\n#   TC-clean — pure clean hook NOT flagged for any check.\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -uo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../bash-hook-lint.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap 'rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR\"' EXIT\n\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks\"\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n\n# === Pattern A (Check 5): M-S51-1 imagined-format grep (literal **Session N**) ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/imagined-format-grep.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -u\nEXEC_FILE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\nLATEST_SESSION=$(grep -E '^\\*\\*Session N\\*\\*:' \"$EXEC_FILE\" | head -1 | sed 's/.*: //' || true)\necho \"$LATEST_SESSION\"\nEOF\n\n# === Pattern B (Check 6): L-S48m-1 CLAUDE_SESSION_ID marker without fallback ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/claude-session-id-marker.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -u\nMARKER_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nMARKER=\"${MARKER_DIR}/.foo-fired-${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID}\"\n[ -f \"$MARKER\" ] && exit 0\ntouch \"$MARKER\"\nEOF\n\n# === Pattern C (Check 7) — TC-C: bare-line grep (orig fixture) ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/pipefail-err-trap-bare-grep.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/log.txt\"\n    grep -m1 -E '^session=' \"$LOG\"\necho \"after grep\"\nEOF\n\n# === Pattern C (Check 7) — TC-C2: command-substitution form (S54 KI-S53-1 regression) ===\n# This is the EXACT shape of session-export-raw.sh:42 pre-S53. Pre-S54 Check 7 missed it.\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/pipefail-cmd-sub-grep.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\nEXEC_FILE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/exec.md\"\nSESSION_N=\"$(grep -E '^foo' \"$EXEC_FILE\" | grep -oE '[0-9]+' | sort -n | tail -1)\"\necho \"$SESSION_N\"\nEOF\n\n# === Pattern C (Check 7) — TC-C3: pipeline grep at start, no || true ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/pipefail-pipeline-grep.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/log.txt\"\ngrep -E 'pat' \"$LOG\" | head -1 | sed 's/foo/bar/'\necho \"done\"\nEOF\n\n# === Pattern C negative — TC-C-cond: `if grep` form NOT flagged ===\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/pipefail-if-grep.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/log.txt\"\nif grep -qE 'foo' \"$LOG\"; M-S52-3 (settings-extract regex truncation at escaped quotes) was caught by hook-firing-counter production smoke writing 0-byte log. Both fixes shipped same-turn; neither bug ever affected production correctness or downstream consumer.\n889\t\n890\t**Rule**: When a firing-test or production smoke catches an author-iteration bug PRE-DEPLOY, that is the SUCCESS path of L-S51-1 empirical-firing discipline — not a failure. Iteration-bugs are the expected output. The metric is \"did any un-caught bug reach production where downstream consumers depended on it?\". Pre-deploy catches don't increment the failure counter.\n891\t\n892\t**Anti-pattern to avoid**: marking an author-iteration cycle as \"failed\" because the first regex was wrong. That confuses the metric. The CORRECT framing: iteration-bug count is the inverse of empirical-firing discipline rigor — more iteration-bugs caught means MORE rigor, not less.\n893\t\n894\t**Correct example** (this turn): both M-S52-2 and M-S52-3 are cataloged with severity LOW (caught pre-deploy via the discipline) — not severity HIGH (which is reserved for un-caught bugs that reached production, like M-S51-1).\n895\t\n896\t**Severity**: medium (framing/calibration rule; affects how subsequent sessions interpret iteration counts).\n897\t\n898\t**Auto-detect**: no — judgment-tier rule; cannot be deterministically detected.\n899\t\n900\t**Where applied**:\n901\t- mistake-log.md § M-S52-2 + M-S52-3 (severity = low per this rule)\n902\t- quality-report 2026-05-05-S52-T3.4-firing-tests.log § Discoveries this turn\n903\t- This entry (L-S52-3)\n904\t\n905\t**Lesson ID**: L-S52-3.\n906\t\n907\t---\n908\t\n909\t### L-S53-1 — Firing-test catches LAYERED latent bugs deeper than upstream detection\n910\t**Date**: 2026-05-05\n911\t**Origin**: S53 T3.4-followup — fixing M-S52-1 (imagined-format) in `session-export-raw.sh` surfaced 2 ADDITIONAL pre-existing latent bugs in the same hook (M-S53-1 pipefail-silent-exit + M-S53-2 archive-prose-anchor-false-positive) that the upstream Pattern A detection layer (`bash-hook-lint.sh` Check 5) did NOT flag.\n912\t\n913\t**Context**: Pattern A (Check 5) at S52 detected the imagined-format symptom (`^\\*\\*Session N\\*\\*:` literal). It did NOT detect:\n914\t- That Method-1's pipefail+ERR-trap silent-exit would prevent Method-2 from ever running (this is L-S48d-1 / Check 7 territory; meta-lint gap noted as KI-S53-1).\n915\t- That Method-1's NEXT-marker grep was unanchored and would false-positive on archive-prose mid-line (S38/S42 NEXT branching gate at line 261 of real `current-execution.md` → SESSION_N=42 returned every export, not the actual latest session).\n916\t\n917\tBoth bugs were surfaced ONLY by the firing-test → production-smoke retrofit cycle:\n918\t- M-S53-1 caught at firing-test TC1 initial fail (no raw-sessions/<DATE>-session-52.md created despite fixed Method-2 grep).\n919\t- M-S53-2 caught at production smoke (raw-sessions filename was `<DATE>-session-42.md` instead of `<DATE>-session-52.md`).\n920\t\n921\t**Rule**: When applying an L-S51-1 retrofit fix to a hook with `set -uo pipefail` + `trap '... ERR'` + bare-grep pipelines, treat the ENTIRE pipeline-success contract as part of the fix scope. A surfaced symptom (imagined-format regex) is rarely the only latent bug — bash discipline failures cluster (pipefail-trap + missing `|| true` + missing line-anchors). Budget for triple-bug discovery per retrofit and fix all surfaced layers in one turn rather than deferring sequentially.\n922\t\n923\t**Anti-pattern to avoid**: ship the \"minimal\" fix matching ONLY the upstream-detected symptom, then defer the deeper layers to a follow-up session. That fragments the firing-test artifact (TC count drifts; coverage diluted) and leaves the hook latently broken in production until the NEXT detection cycle catches it.\n924\t\n925\t**Correct example** (this turn): S53 retrofit fixed all 3 layered bugs (M-S52-1 + M-S53-1 + M-S53-2) in one Edit + one firing-test extension (5 → 7 TCs) + one production smoke. Anchor-on-grep is NOT yet a check; consider adding \"Check 8 unanchored-`^`-on-positional-grep\" in S54+ promote-rule cycle.\n930\t\n931\t**Where applied**:\n932\t- `scripts/hooks/session-export-raw.sh` triple-bug fix\n933\t- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/session-export-raw-fire-test.sh` 7 TCs (TC6 + TC7 added for M-S53-* coverage)\n934\t- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S53-T3.4-followup-fixes.log` § Triple-bug discovery\n935\t- This entry (L-S53-1)\n936\t\n937\t**Lesson ID**: L-S53-1.\n938\t\n939\t---\n940\t\n941\t### L-S53-2 — Autonomous loop reliability: idempotency must advance per Stop-event, not per-SessionStart-tick\n942\t**Date**: 2026-05-05\n943\t**Origin**: S53 post-checkpoint follow-up — user pushback \"sao lại không autonomous run tiếp? tại sao, harness lại lỗi à\" surfaced harness reliability gap. M-S53-3 root cause: continue-injector per-SessionStart-tick idempotency blocked autonomous Stop→Mode-D→continue-injector loop within a single session.\n944\t\n945\t**Context**: L-S49b-4 (PRIMARY rule) says LLM ends turn after checkpoint write; Mode-D in autonomous-stop-watchdog.sh fires continue-injector to type \"continue\" into TUI → fresh resume. The mechanism worked end-to-end UP TO injector silent-exit. continue-injector.ps1 had two idempotency layers:\n946\t- 60s rate-limit (sufficient for L-S48-1 burst-spam scenario where 180 spawns occurred over a morning ~30-40min apart)\n947\t- Per-SessionStart-tick marker `.continue-fired-{SessionTag}` where SessionTag = `.session-ready` mtime ticks\n948\t\n949\t`.session-ready` only updates on SessionStart events. Within a session, SessionTag NEVER changes. After the FIRST injector fire for a given SessionStart, ALL subsequent fires (including legitimate Mode-D handoffs) hit \"already fired for this session-ready tick\" silent exit.\n950\t\n951\t**Rule**: Idempotency for autonomous-loop hooks must advance on the CAUSAL event (Stop, checkpoint write, recovery trigger) — NOT on the SessionStart tick. SessionStart is a coarse-grained lifecycle event that fires only on `/clear` / fresh-launch / `--resume`; it cannot advance within an active session, so any idempotency tied to it permanently blocks intra-session autonomous chains.\n952\t\n953\t**Anti-pattern to avoid**: layering \"belt-and-suspenders\" idempotency without analyzing whether the redundant layer can BLOCK the legitimate flow. L-S48-1 added per-tick marker as defense-in-depth; the 60s rate-limit was already sufficient for the original spam scenario (spawns 30-40 min apart). The redundant per-tick marker silently disabled the autonomous loop the harness exists to deliver.\n954\t\n955\t**Correct example** (post-S53 contract):\n956\t- 60s rate-limit (cross-caller spam protection): retained\n957\t- Per-event markers on caller side:\n958\t  - `bootstrap` (SessionStart): one fire per SessionStart (bootstrap itself only fires once per event)\n959\t  - `Mode-A` (API-truncation): `.api-truncation-recovery-fired-$RECOVERY_KEY`\n960\t  - `Mode-C` (premature wind-down): `.mode-c-recovery-fired-$MODE_C_KEY`\n961\t  - `Mode-D` (clean handoff): `.mode-d-recovery-fired-$MODE_D_KEY`\n962\t- Per-tick marker on injector side: REMOVED (M-S53-3 fix)\n963\t\n964\t**Severity**: HIGH (autonomous-full mode loop dead until fix lands; user-visible reliability gap directly contradicting AOM autonomous-loop guarantees).\n965\t\n966\t**Auto-detect**: yes — propose harness-health-self-scan signal HH-X (NEW): \"if continue-injector log shows `already fired for this session-ready tick` AND `.mode-d-recovery-fired-*` marker exists for same time window → flag as M-S53-3-recurrence\". SessionStart is a coarse-grained lifecycle event that fires only on `/clear` / fresh-launch / `--resume`; it cannot advance within an active session, so any idempotency tied to it permanently blocks intra-session autonomous chains.\n\n**Anti-pattern to avoid**: layering \"belt-and-suspenders\" idempotency without analyzing whether the redundant layer can BLOCK the legitimate flow. L-S48-1 added per-tick marker as defense-in-depth; the 60s rate-limit was already sufficient for the original spam scenario (spawns 30-40 min apart). The redundant per-tick marker silently disabled the autonomous loop the harness exists to deliver.\n\n**Correct example** (post-S53 contract):\n- 60s rate-limit (cross-caller spam protection): retained\n- Per-event markers on caller side:\n  - `bootstrap` (SessionStart): one fire per SessionStart (bootstrap itself only fires once per event)\n  - `Mode-A` (API-truncation): `.api-truncation-recovery-fired-$RECOVERY_KEY`\n  - `Mode-C` (premature wind-down): `.mode-c-recovery-fired-$MODE_C_KEY`\n  - `Mode-D` (clean handoff): `.mode-d-recovery-fired-$MODE_D_KEY`\n- Per-tick marker on injector side: REMOVED (M-S53-3 fix)\n\n**Severity**: HIGH (autonomous-full mode loop dead until fix lands; user-visible reliability gap directly contradicting AOM autonomous-loop guarantees).\n\n**Auto-detect**: yes — propose harness-health-self-scan signal HH-X (NEW): \"if continue-injector log shows `already fired for this session-ready tick` AND `.mode-d-recovery-fired-*` marker exists for same time window → flag as M-S53-3-recurrence\". Codify in Phase 3.5 T6 hook.\n\n**Status of L-S48-1**: SUPERSEDED IN PART. Per-tick marker removed. Original L-S48-1 cross-window-typing prevention via `Try-FocusClaudeTerminal` ancestor-walk is RETAINED (still essential to prevent typing into wrong window).\n\n**Where applied**:\n- `scripts/hooks/continue-injector.ps1` lines 114-134 (per-tick marker block REMOVED; explanatory comment retained for forensic clarity)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md` § M-S53-3\n- This entry (L-S53-2)\n- Phase 3.5 T6 hook design will reference this as HH-X auto-detect signal\n","numLines":96,"startLine":880,"totalLines":977}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7e5a7bec-3ce6-4e06-a0d3-85a4a4e5e366","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"6fa1c8c7-7014-4741-9fb0-2edf9459f4f1","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"81839d4b-18bd-4472-8254-705ffb4354ba","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01TBewisYMkwUEdaUWUb37ag","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Append L-S54-1 + L-S54-2 + KI status updates vào agent-notes."}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4029,"cache_read_input_tokens":165849,"output_tokens":4133,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":4029,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":4133,"cache_read_input_tokens":165849,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4029,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":4029},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CajXAtGe8sDq9xfbtogi5","type":"assistant","uuid":"2c7ab779-6947-426b-94ae-350c13b6eae0","timestamp":"2026-05-05T13:43:08.337Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"6fa1c8c7-7014-4741-9fb0-2edf9459f4f1","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"2c7ab779-6947-426b-94ae-350c13b6eae0","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01TBewisYMkwUEdaUWUb37ag","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01CbgW6LgwygnLMgdTSbWcNp","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\memory\\agent-notes.md","old_string":"**Where applied**:\n- `scripts/hooks/continue-injector.ps1` lines 114-134 (per-tick marker block REMOVED; explanatory comment retained for forensic clarity)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md` § M-S53-3\n- This entry (L-S53-2)\n- Phase 3.5 T6 hook design will reference this as HH-X auto-detect signal","new_string":"**Where applied**:\n- `scripts/hooks/continue-injector.ps1` lines 114-134 (per-tick marker block REMOVED; explanatory comment retained for forensic clarity)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md` § M-S53-3\n- This entry (L-S53-2)\n- Phase 3.5 T6 hook design will reference this as HH-X auto-detect signal\n\n---\n\n### L-S54-1 — 2-pass grep filter beats single-regex with `[^^]` first-char negation when target token CAN be body start\n**Date**: 2026-05-05\n**Origin**: S54 Check 8 authoring — initial Check 8 regex used `['\\\"][^^'\\\"][^'\\\"]*(MARKER)` to \"skip patterns starting with `^`\". Failed TC-D firing-test because when MARKER is the FIRST body char (e.g. `'S[0-9]+ NEXT'`), the `[^^'\\\"]` consumes `S` and the trailing alternation never finds another `S\\[0-9\\]\\+` token to match.\n\n**Context**: Tempting to encode \"first char must NOT be caret\" as a single regex constraint. But ERE has no lookahead — the negation char-class `[^^]` consumes a char, and that char is unavailable for downstream alternation. Backtracking can't recover because the consumed `[^^]` position is fixed.\n\n**Rule**: When a heuristic needs to (a) detect a routing/marker token in pattern body AND (b) exclude patterns where body starts with a specific anchor char, use 2-pass filter:\n- pass-1: `grep -E \"marker_token_regex\"` finds candidate lines\n- pass-2: `grep -vE \"anchor_prefix_regex\"` excludes anchored patterns\n\nThis is cleaner, more readable, and avoids ERE backtracking pitfalls. Applies to any heuristic involving \"find pattern X but exclude when prefix Y exists\".\n\n**Anti-pattern to avoid**: trying to encode the entire whitelist + flag logic as a single grep regex. Single-regex bash heuristics become hairy fast and fail in non-obvious ways (token-at-body-start edge case here was a textbook example).\n\n**Correct example** (S54 Check 8):\n```bash\nBAD_D=\"$(grep -nE \"grep[[:space:]]+(...)?['\\\"][^'\\\"]*(S\\[0-9\\]\\+|...)\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null \\\n  | grep -vE \"grep[[:space:]]+(...)?['\\\"]\\^\" \\\n  | grep -vE '^[[:space:]]*[0-9]+:[[:space:]]*#' \\\n  || true)\"\n```\n\n**Severity**: medium (technique rule; affects future bash-lint authoring + any deterministic pattern-detection hook).\n\n**Auto-detect**: no — judgment-tier rule; lint-on-lint would be over-meta. Apply at code-review time during firing-test author cycle.\n\n**Where applied**:\n- `scripts/hooks/bash-hook-lint.sh` Check 8 (post-iteration v2; v1 used `[^^][^...]*` and failed TC-D)\n- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/bash-hook-lint-fire-test.sh` TC-D / TC-D-anchored / TC-D-content (pass-1+pass-2 approach validated empirically)\n- This entry (L-S54-1)\n\n**Lesson ID**: L-S54-1.\n\n---\n\n### L-S54-2 — Meta-lint heuristic upgrade reveals BROADER retrofit scope than estimate\n**Date**: 2026-05-05\n**Origin**: S54 production smoke — refined Check 7 (now catching command-sub + pipeline forms in addition to bare-line) surfaced 27 latent L-S48d-1 candidates. Pre-S54 Check 7 surfaced 0 hits in the same hook tree. Same with Check 8: 4 unanchored-positional-grep candidates.\n\n**Context**: Phase 2.5 (HH-A..HH-H) closed \"ritually\" 8/8 GREEN. Phase 3.5 master-plan (010) estimated T7 retrofit scope as \"~22 remaining HH-A..HH-H untested hooks\" based on session-count audit. S54 production smoke shows the L-S48d-1 family alone has ≥27 hooks with un-guarded grep under pipefail+ERR-trap context. The actual retrofit surface is BROADER than the original count-by-session estimate.\n\n**Rule**: When a deterministic detector heuristic is upgraded mid-phase, expect production-smoke counts to JUMP, often substantially. Treat the new count as the empirical baseline for retrofit scope (not the prior under-detected count). Update master-plan envelope estimates downstream.\n\n**Anti-pattern to avoid**: treat the post-upgrade count as \"regression\" or \"noise\". The detector wasn't catching real bugs before; the higher count is the truth surfaced. Resist the urge to relax the heuristic to bring counts back down.\n\n**Correct example** (S54 → S55+ implications):\n- T7 (firing-tests retrofit) scope reframes: each hook retrofit must ALSO normalize grep-guard form per L-S48d-1 + audit positional-marker patterns for `^` anchor per L-S53-2 — not just \"add a firing-test\".\n- 010 plan envelope estimate for T7 batch should bump to account for grep-normalization step per hook.\n- Promote-rule cycle should treat lint upgrade as observable signal of phase-2.5 incompleteness (not Phase 2.5 success criterion violation; just empirical correction).\n\n**Severity**: medium (planning/scoping rule; affects T7 phase budget; informs how phase-closure rituals interpret deterministic-gate counts).\n\n**Auto-detect**: yes — every post-upgrade lint smoke run logs the count delta. Compare today's `bash-hook-lint WARN N violation(s)` to last archived count; flag if N changed by ≥5 OR if a new violation code appeared.\n\n**Where applied**:\n- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S54-bash-hook-lint-meta-refinement.log` § Production smoke breakdown\n- `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md` S54 row (T7 scope reframing note)\n- This entry (L-S54-2)\n\n**Lesson ID**: L-S54-2.\n\n---\n\n### KI-S54-1 — Check 7 recognizes only canonical `|| true` / `|| :` guards (deferred refinement)\n**Date**: 2026-05-05\n**Origin**: S54 Check 7 refinement — chose conservative recognition of `|| true` and `|| :` as ONLY canonical guards. Alternative forms `|| echo \"...\"`, `|| exit 0`, `|| return 0` flag as Check 7 false-positive even though they semantically guard against the ERR trap.\n\n**Status**: deferred refinement — accepted trade-off. Hook authors should normalize to canonical `|| true; ...` form for clarity. If false-positive rate proves disruptive in S55+ batch retrofit (≥5 hooks legitimately use alternative-guard form and complain), promote refinement to add `|| (echo|exit|return)` recognition.\n\n**Where tracked**:\n- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S54-bash-hook-lint-meta-refinement.log` § Lessons table\n- This entry (KI-S54-1)\n\n---\n\n### KI-S52-1 + KI-S53-1 — CLOSED via S54 Check 7 refinement + Check 8 NEW\n**Date**: 2026-05-05 (closure ratification)\n**Closed by**: S54 path (c) — bash-hook-lint Check 7+8 heuristic refinement.\n\n- **KI-S53-1** (open at S53 close): Check 7 missed `session-export-raw.sh` despite textbook L-S48d-1 case. CLOSED — Check 7 now catches command-substitution + pipeline + bare-line forms uniformly via 3-form-aware heuristic. Production smoke proves: 27 hooks now flagged (was 0 pre-S54).\n- **KI-S52-1** (open at S52 close): no Check covered unanchored-positional-marker grep family that produced M-S53-2. CLOSED — NEW Check 8 (Pattern D) ships with 2-pass filter (find marker token → exclude `^`-anchored). Production smoke proves: 4 hooks flagged.\n\nBoth closures verified empirically via 11/11 firing-test PASS + production smoke + spot-check of 3 already-fixed hooks (session-export-raw.sh + session-start-bootstrap.sh + promotion-cycle-trigger.sh — still flagged because OTHER greps in same files lack canonical `|| true` guard, expected per per-file Check 7 design)."},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4029,"cache_read_input_tokens":165849,"output_tokens":4133,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":4029,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":4133,"cache_read_input_tokens":165849,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4029,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":4029},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CajXAtGe8sDq9xfbtogi5","type":"assistant","uuid":"1cdaac18-2070-4e23-a555-0f3db5f05a0c","timestamp":"2026-05-05T13:43:58.376Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"6fa1c8c7-7014-4741-9fb0-2edf9459f4f1","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Apply at code-review time during firing-test author cycle.\n\n**Where applied**:\n- `scripts/hooks/bash-hook-lint.sh` Check 8 (post-iteration v2; v1 used `[^^][^...]*` and failed TC-D)\n- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/bash-hook-lint-fire-test.sh` TC-D / TC-D-anchored / TC-D-content (pass-1+pass-2 approach validated empirically)\n- This entry (L-S54-1)\n\n**Lesson ID**: L-S54-1.\n\n---\n\n### L-S54-2 — Meta-lint heuristic upgrade reveals BROADER retrofit scope than estimate\n**Date**: 2026-05-05\n**Origin**: S54 production smoke — refined Check 7 (now catching command-sub + pipeline forms in addition to bare-line) surfaced 27 latent L-S48d-1 candidates. Pre-S54 Check 7 surfaced 0 hits in the same hook tree. Same with Check 8: 4 unanchored-positional-grep candidates.\n\n**Context**: Phase 2.5 (HH-A..HH-H) closed \"ritually\" 8/8 GREEN. Phase 3.5 master-plan (010) estimated T7 retrofit scope as \"~22 remaining HH-A..HH-H untested hooks\" based on session-count audit. S54 production smoke shows the L-S48d-1 family alone has ≥27 hooks with un-guarded grep under pipefail+ERR-trap context. The actual retrofit surface is BROADER than the original count-by-session estimate.\n\n**Rule**: When a deterministic detector heuristic is upgraded mid-phase, expect production-smoke counts to JUMP, often substantially. Treat the new count as the empirical baseline for retrofit scope (not the prior under-detected count). Update master-plan envelope estimates downstream.\n\n**Anti-pattern to avoid**: treat the post-upgrade count as \"regression\" or \"noise\". The detector wasn't catching real bugs before; the higher count is the truth surfaced. Resist the urge to relax the heuristic to bring counts back down.\n\n**Correct example** (S54 → S55+ implications):\n- T7 (firing-tests retrofit) scope reframes: each hook retrofit must ALSO normalize grep-guard form per L-S48d-1 + audit positional-marker patterns for `^` anchor per L-S53-2 — not just \"add a firing-test\".\n- 010 plan envelope estimate for T7 batch should bump to account for grep-normalization step per hook.\n- Promote-rule cycle should treat lint upgrade as observable signal of phase-2.5 incompleteness (not Phase 2.5 success criterion violation; just empirical correction).\n\n**Severity**: medium (planning/scoping rule; affects T7 phase budget; informs how phase-closure rituals interpret deterministic-gate counts).\n\n**Auto-detect**: yes — every post-upgrade lint smoke run logs the count delta. Compare today's `bash-hook-lint WARN N violation(s)` to last archived count; flag if N changed by ≥5 OR if a new violation code appeared.\n\n**Where applied**:\n- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S54-bash-hook-lint-meta-refinement.log` § Production smoke breakdown\n- `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md` S54 row (T7 scope reframing note)\n- This entry (L-S54-2)\n\n**Lesson ID**: L-S54-2.\n\n---\n\n### KI-S54-1 — Check 7 recognizes only canonical `|| true` / `|| :` guards (deferred refinement)\n**Date**: 2026-05-05\n**Origin**: S54 Check 7 refinement — chose conservative recognition of `|| true` and `|| :` as ONLY canonical guards. Alternative forms `|| echo \"...\"`, `|| exit 0`, `|| return 0` flag as Check 7 false-positive even though they semantically guard against the ERR trap.\n\n**Status**: deferred refinement — accepted trade-off. Hook authors should normalize to canonical `|| true; ...` form for clarity. Tightened assertion regex from `${2}.*${3}` to `${2}: ${3}\\.sh ` to prevent substring collisions (e.g. \"anchored\" inside \"unanchored\").\n4. **Write** `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S54-bash-hook-lint-meta-refinement.log` — full TC results table + production-smoke 38-violation breakdown (27 L-S48d-1 + 4 L-S53-2 NEW-detected).\n\n**Firing-test result**: 11/11 PASS (final iteration; baseline was 4/4 → first refinement attempt 10/11 with TC-D fail → assertion regex fix → 11/11 PASS).\n\n**Production smoke discoveries**:\n- **27 latent L-S48d-1 candidates** surfaced (was 0 pre-S54 via old narrow heuristic). Per-file Check 7 design (one violation per file regardless of grep count) is correct.\n- **4 latent L-S53-2 candidates** surfaced via NEW Check 8: autonomous-stop-watchdog.sh + promotion-cycle-trigger.sh + session-start-bootstrap.sh + stale-prompt-detector.sh.\n- 6 pre-existing L-S11-1 + 1 D-IDENTITY false-positive (autonomous-protocol.md line 30 doctrine-affirming legit-quote, lint heuristic too aggressive) — both deferred backlog, not S54 scope.\n\n**Mid-S54 iteration-bugs caught at firing-test stage** (per L-S52-3 doctrine — SUCCESS path of L-S51-1, not failure; not catalogued as M-S54-X):\n- Iter-1: Check 8 regex used `[^^][^'\\\"]*(MARKER)` which fails when MARKER = first body char (TC-D fail). → Refined to 2-pass filter (codified as L-S54-1).\n- Iter-2: assertion regex `${2}.*${3}` substring-matched \"anchored\" inside \"unanchored\" (TC-D-anchored false-positive). → Tightened to `: ${3}\\.sh ` boundary.\n\n**Lessons NEW**: L-S54-1 (2-pass grep filter beats single-regex `[^^]` for token-at-body-start cases) + L-S54-2 (meta-lint heuristic upgrade reveals BROADER retrofit scope: 27 vs estimated ~22; T7 envelope reframes). **KI deferred**: KI-S54-1 (Check 7 doesn't recognize alternative guards `|| echo`/`|| exit`/`|| return`; conservative `|| true|:` is canonical). **KI CLOSED**: KI-S52-1 + KI-S53-1.\n\n**S54 self-track**: ~90-110K main this turn (5 SessionStart reads + 3 hook source VBW + 1 firing-test VBW + 1 mistake-log read + 3 Edits to bash-hook-lint + 1 Write+1 Edit to firing-test + 3 firing-test invocations + 1 production smoke + 1 quality report write + memory updates + checkpoint). 0 subagent burn this turn (main-session per L-S43f-2). Tightened assertion regex from `${2}.*${3}` to `${2}: ${3}\\.sh ` to prevent substring collisions (e.g. \"anchored\" inside \"unanchored\").\n4. **Write** `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S54-bash-hook-lint-meta-refinement.log` — full TC results table + production-smoke 38-violation breakdown (27 L-S48d-1 + 4 L-S53-2 NEW-detected).\n\n**Firing-test result**: 11/11 PASS (final iteration; baseline was 4/4 → first refinement attempt 10/11 with TC-D fail → assertion regex fix → 11/11 PASS).\n\n**Production smoke discoveries**:\n- **27 latent L-S48d-1 candidates** surfaced (was 0 pre-S54 via old narrow heuristic).
